I'll use a regex to find sequences where a closing `</hp:t></hp:run>` is immediately followed by an opening `<hp:run ...><hp:t>`. By removing these intermediate tags, I can merge the text content back into a single node.\n\nThis pre-processing step is crucial. Reads patient_intake.json\n2. Normalizes phone number to 000-0000-0000 format\n3. Calculates Korean age (만 나이) as of visit date\n4. Replaces placeholders in the HWPX template\n5. Removes layout cache elements\n6. Produces clinic_intake_ready.hwpx\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport zipfile\nimport shutil\nimport os\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n\ndef parse_date(date_str):\n    \"\"\"Parse date string in YYYY-MM-DD format.\"\"\"\n    return datetime.strptime(date_str, \"%Y-%m-%d\")\n\n\ndef normalize_phone(phone_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize phone number to 000-0000-0000 format.\n    Extracts digits only and formats as 3-4-4.\n    \"\"\"\n    digits = re.sub(r'\\D', '', phone_str)\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:]}\"\n    elif len(digits) == 10:\n        return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:]}\"\n    else:\n        # For other lengths, try to format reasonably\n        if len(digits) <= 3:\n            return digits\n        elif len(digits) <= 7:\n            return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:]}\"\n        else:\n            return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:]}\"\n\n\ndef calculate_korean_age(birth_date_str, visit_date_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Korean 만 나이 (full-year age) as of the visit date.\n\n    Age = visit_year - birth_year, minus 1 if visit is before birthday that year.\n    Returns the age number.\n    \"\"\"\n    birth_date = parse_date(birth_date_str)\n    visit_date = parse_date(visit_date_str)\n\n    age = visit_date.year - birth_date.year\n\n    # Subtract 1 if the visit date is before the birthday in that year\n    if (visit_date.month, visit_date.day) < (birth_date.month, birth_date.day):\n        age -= 1\n\n    return age\n\n\ndef collapse_split_placeholders(xml_string):\n    \"\"\"\n    Collapse placeholders that may be split across multiple <hp:t> tags.\n\n    HWPX can split text like {{placeholder}} across multiple run/text elements\n    due to internal formatting. This function merges them back together.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern to find split tag boundaries between potential placeholder parts\n    # We look for sequences of </hp:t></hp:run><hp:run...><hp:t> and remove them\n    # to merge adjacent text content\n\n    # First, let's find regions around {{ and }} and collapse them\n    # We'll do this iteratively to handle nested splits\n\n    # Pattern to match the boundary between text elements\n    boundary_pattern = r'</hp:t>\\s*</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>\\s*<hp:t>'\n\n    # Keep collapsing until no more changes (to handle chained splits)\n    max_iterations = 100\n    for _ in range(max_iterations):\n        new_xml = re.sub(boundary_pattern, '', xml_string)\n        if new_xml == xml_string:\n            break\n        xml_string = new_xml\n\n    return xml_string\n\n\ndef remove_line_seg_arrays(xml_string):\n    \"\"\"\n    Remove all <hp:lineSegArray>...</hp:lineSegArray> elements.\n    These are layout caches that should be stripped when text is modified.\n    \"\"\"\n    return re.sub(r'<hp:lineSegArray>.*?</hp:lineSegArray>', '', xml_string, flags=re.DOTALL)\n\n\ndef process_hwpx():\n    \"\"\"Main processing function.\"\"\"\n\n    # Step 2: Process phone number\n    raw_phone = patient_data.get('회신전화', '')\n    normalized_phone = normalize_phone(raw_phone)\n    print(f\"  Phone: {raw_phone} -> {normalized_phone}\")\n\n    # Step 3: Calculate Korean age\n    birth_date = patient_data.get('생년월일', '')\n    visit_date = patient_data.get('방문일', '')\n    age = calculate_korean_age(birth_date, visit_date)\n    age_note = f\"({age}세)\"\n    print(f\"  Birth: {birth_date}, Visit: {visit_date}, Age: {age}\")\n\n    # Step 4: Build replacement mapping\n    replacements = {\n        '{{환자명}}': patient_data.get('환자명', ''),\n        '{{생년월일}}': patient_data.get('생년월일', '') + age_note,\n        '{{방문일}}': patient_data.get('방문일', ''),\n        '{{진료팀}}': patient_data.get('진료팀', ''),\n        '{{회신전화}}': normalized_phone,\n        '{{증상요약}}': patient_data.get('증상요약', ''),\n        '{{보험상태}}': patient_data.get('보험상태', ''),\n    }\n\n    print(\"  Replacements:\")\n    for key, value in replacements.items():\n        print(f\"    {key} -> {value}\")\n\n    # Copy template to output\n    shutil.copy(template_path, output_path)\n    print(f\"  Copied template to {output_path}\")\n\n    # Read the original XML content\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_path, 'r') as zin:\n        xml_content = zin.read('Contents/section0.xml').decode('utf-8')\n\n    # Step 6: Collapse split placeholders\n    print(\"Step 6: Collapsing split placeholders...\")\n    original_xml = xml_content\n    xml_content = collapse_split_placeholders(xml_content)\n    if xml_content != original_xml:\n        print(\"  Collapsed some split placeholders\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  No split placeholders found\")\n\n    # Step 7: Perform replacements\n    print(\"Step 7: Performing placeholder replacements...\")\n    for placeholder, value in replacements.items():\n        if placeholder in xml_content:\n            xml_content = xml_content.replace(placeholder, value)\n            print(f\"  Replaced {placeholder}\")\n        else:\n            print(f\"  WARNING: {placeholder} not found in XML\")\n\n    # Step 8: Remove lineSegArray elements\n    print(\"Step 8: Removing lineSegArray elements...\")\n    xml_content = remove_line_seg_arrays(xml_content)\n    print(\"  Removed all lineSegArray elements\")\n\n    # Write modified XML\n                    zout.writestr(item, xml_content.encode('utf-8'))\n                else:\n                    # Copy other files as-is\n                    zout.writestr(item, zin.read(item))\n\n    # Replace original with temp\n    shutil.move(temp_path, output_path)\n    print(f\"  Updated {output_path}\")\n\n    # Step 10: Validation\n    print(\"\\nStep 10: Validation...\")\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'r') as z:\n        validated_xml = z.read('Contents/section0.xml').decode('utf-8')\n\n    checks_passed = 0\n    checks_failed = 0\n\n    # Check 2: Age note appears\n    if age_note in validated_xml:\n        print(f\"  PASS: Age note '{age_note}' found\")\n        checks_passed += 1\n    else:\n        print(f\"  FAIL: Age note '{age_note}' not found\")\n        checks_failed += 1\n\n    # Check 3: Phone number in correct format\n    if normalized_phone in validated_xml:\n        print(f\"  PASS: Phone number '{normalized_phone}' found\")\n        checks_passed += 1\n    else:\n        print(f\"  FAIL: Phone number '{normalized_phone}' not found\")\n        checks_failed += 1\n\n    # Reads patient_intake.json\n2. Normalizes phone number to 000-0000-0000 format\n3. Calculates Korean age (만 나이) as of visit date\n4. Replaces placeholders in the HWPX template\n5. Removes layout cache elements\n6. Produces clinic_intake_ready.hwpx\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport zipfile\nimport shutil\nimport os\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\n\ndef parse_date(date_str):\n    \"\"\"Parse date string in YYYY-MM-DD format.\"\"\"\n    return datetime.strptime(date_str, \"%Y-%m-%d\")\n\n\ndef normalize_phone(phone_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Normalize phone number to 000-0000-0000 format.\n    Extracts digits only and formats as 3-4-4.\n    \"\"\"\n    digits = re.sub(r'\\D', '', phone_str)\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:]}\"\n    elif len(digits) == 10:\n        return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:]}\"\n    else:\n        # For other lengths, try to format reasonably\n        if len(digits) <= 3:\n            return digits\n        elif len(digits) <= 7:\n            return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:]}\"\n        else:\n            return f\"{digits[:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:]}\"\n\n\ndef calculate_korean_age(birth_date_str, visit_date_str):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Korean 만 나이 (full-year age) as of the visit date.\n\n    Age = visit_year - birth_year, minus 1 if visit is before birthday that year.\n    Returns the age number.\n    \"\"\"\n    birth_date = parse_date(birth_date_str)\n    visit_date = parse_date(visit_date_str)\n\n    age = visit_date.year - birth_date.year\n\n    # Subtract 1 if the visit date is before the birthday in that year\n    if (visit_date.month, visit_date.day) < (birth_date.month, birth_date.day):\n        age -= 1\n\n    return age\n\n\ndef collapse_split_placeholders(xml_string):\n    \"\"\"\n    Collapse placeholders that may be split across multiple <hp:t> tags.\n\n    HWPX can split text like {{placeholder}} across multiple run/text elements\n    due to internal formatting. This function merges them back together.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern to find split tag boundaries between potential placeholder parts\n    # We look for sequences of </hp:t></hp:run><hp:run...><hp:t> and remove them\n    # to merge adjacent text content\n\n    # First, let's find regions around {{ and }} and collapse them\n    # We'll do this iteratively to handle nested splits\n\n    # Pattern to match the boundary between text elements\n    boundary_pattern = r'</hp:t>\\s*</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>\\s*<hp:t>'\n\n    #
